<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-94F6515A-3D31-4CC3-B8C6-9C2867CCD37D'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Find the difference between $43 \dfrac{1}{3}$ and $51 \dfrac{2}{3}$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 51 \dfrac {2} {3} - 43 \dfrac {1} {3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {155} {3} - \dfrac {130} {3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {155 - 130} {3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {25} {3} \\\\
&= 8 \dfrac {1} {3}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$8 \dfrac {1} {3}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 51 \dfrac {2} {3} - 43 \dfrac {1} {3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {155} {3} - \dfrac {130} {3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {155 - 130} {3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {25} {3} \\\\
&= 8 \dfrac {1} {3}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$8 \dfrac {1} {3}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-F5BB2E80-58A6-4ACA-80C3-EEBED0513A81'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is a half of three-quarters?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \text{half of three-quarters} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times \dfrac {3} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {2 \times 4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {8}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {3}{8}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \text{half of three-quarters} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times \dfrac {3} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {2 \times 4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {8}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac {3}{8}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

$\dfrac{2}{3} - \dfrac{1}{6}$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {2} {3} - \dfrac {1} {6} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 2} {3 \times 2} - \dfrac {1} {6} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4} {6} - \dfrac {1} {6} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4 - 1} {6} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {6} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {1} {2}$

</div>

&= \dfrac {4 - 1} {6} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {6} \\\\

\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-80F59E43-EAF8-4829-9B47-980C2D298D21'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is $\dfrac {2} {7}$ of $315$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {2} {7} \text { of } 315 \\\\
&= \dfrac {2} {7} \times 315 \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 315} {7}   \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 45 \times 7} {7}   \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 45 \times \cancel{7}} {\cancel{7}}   \\\\
&= 2 \times 45  \\\\
&= 90
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$90$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {2} {7} \text { of } 315 \\\\
&= \dfrac {2} {7} \times 315 \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 315} {7}   \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 45 \times 7} {7}   \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 45 \times \cancel{7}} {\cancel{7}}   \\\\
&= 2 \times 45  \\\\
&= 90
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-2094CB63-4A8B-4A7D-935F-06B130312908'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

In a bag of eleven marbles five of them are green, two are yellow and the rest are purple.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What fraction of the marbles are purple?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Fraction} &= \dfrac {\text {Purple marbles}} {\text {Total number of marbles}} \\\\
&= \dfrac {11 - (5 + 2)} {11} \\\\
&= \dfrac {11 - 7} {11} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4} {11}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {4} {11}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Fraction} &= \dfrac {\text {Purple marbles}} {\text {Total number of marbles}} \\\\
&= \dfrac {11 - (5 + 2)} {11} \\\\
&= \dfrac {11 - 7} {11} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4} {11}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac {4} {11}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

If the two yellow marbles are both removed, what fraction of the 
remaining marbles are green?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Fraction} &= \dfrac {\text {Green marbles}} {\text {Remaining marbles}} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5} {11 - 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5} {9}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {5} {9}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

&= \dfrac {5} {11 - 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5} {9}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='workings'>

&= \dfrac {8} {24} \\\\
&= \dfrac {8} {8 \times 3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {8}} {\cancel {8} \times 3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {3}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {1} {3}$

</div>
</div>

&= \dfrac {\cancel {8}} {\cancel {8} \times 3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {3}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-B5B49362-C409-4628-B777-B5B39EC20042'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What fraction of a minute is $40 \ \text{seconds}$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

  
There are $60 \ \text{seconds}$ in a minute, so

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac{40}{60} \\\\
&= \dfrac{20 \times 2}{20 \times 3} \\\\
&= \dfrac{\cancel{20} \times 2}{\cancel{20} \times 3} \\\\
&= \dfrac{2}{3}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac{2}{3}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>  
There are $60 \ \text{seconds}$ in a minute, so

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac{40}{60} \\\\
&= \dfrac{20 \times 2}{20 \times 3} \\\\
&= \dfrac{\cancel{20} \times 2}{\cancel{20} \times 3} \\\\
&= \dfrac{2}{3}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac{2}{3}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-29315613-031C-43A5-881D-8518DC15DE73'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What fraction of this diagram is shaded?

![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg)


</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total triangles}  &= 16   \\\\
\text{Shaded}           &= 3    \\\\
\text{Fraction shaded}  &= \dfrac {3} {16}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {3} {16}$

</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-FE2AB576-06F8-49D8-AF99-928322211446'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Find $\dfrac{3}{8}$ of $48$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {3} {8} \text{ of } 48 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {8} \times 48 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 48} {8} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 6 \times 8} {8} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 6 \times \cancel{8}} {\cancel{8}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 6 \\\\
&= 18
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$18$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {3} {8} \text{ of } 48 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {8} \times 48 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 48} {8} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 6 \times 8} {8} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 6 \times \cancel{8}} {\cancel{8}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 6 \\\\
&= 18
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$18$
</code></pre>
</div>

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Find $\dfrac{1}{7}$ of $35$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {7} \text{ of } 35 \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {7} \times 35 \\\\
&= \dfrac {35} {7} \\\\
&= \dfrac { 5 \times 7} {7} \\\\
&= \dfrac { 5 \times \cancel{7}} {\cancel{7}} \\\\
&= 5
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$5$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {7} \text{ of } 35 \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {7} \times 35 \\\\
&= \dfrac {35} {7} \\\\
&= \dfrac { 5 \times 7} {7} \\\\
&= \dfrac { 5 \times \cancel{7}} {\cancel{7}} \\\\
&= 5
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$5$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Find $\dfrac{3}{7}$ of $35$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {3} {7} \text{ of } 35 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {7} \times 35 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 35} {7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 5 \times 7} {7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 5 \times \cancel{7}} {\cancel{7}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 5  \\\\
&= 15
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$15$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {3} {7} \text{ of } 35 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {7} \times 35 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 35} {7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 5 \times 7} {7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 5 \times \cancel{7}} {\cancel{7}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 5  \\\\
&= 15
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>

<div class='question question'>

Circle all the numbers below that are more than $\dfrac{1}{4}$?

$0.2  \quad \dfrac{1}{5}  \quad 0.4  \quad \dfrac{3}{8}  \quad 0.027$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$\dfrac {1} {4} = 0.25$

| Number            |  Decimal          |  $> 0.25$    |
|:-------:          |:-----------------:|:------------:|
| $0.2$             | $0.2$             | No           |
| $\dfrac{1}{5}$    | $0.2$             | No           |
| $0.4$             | $0.4$             | Yes          |
| $\dfrac{3}{8}$    | $0.375$           | Yes          |
| $0.027$           | $0.027$           | No           |


Based on the calculation above,

Numbers more than $\dfrac {1} {4} = 0.4, \dfrac {3} {8}$

</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-7A748D7C-3D23-4AF2-9630-1A0D40C6DB68'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is $\dfrac{3}{7}$  of $385$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {3} {7} \ \text {of} \ 385 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {7} \times 385 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 385} {7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 55 \times 7} {7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 55 \times \cancel {7}} {\cancel {7}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 55 \\\\
&= 165
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$165$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {3} {7} \ \text {of} \ 385 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {7} \times 385 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 385} {7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 55 \times 7} {7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 55 \times \cancel {7}} {\cancel {7}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 55 \\\\
&= 165
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-E709EA37-048F-4696-A4BE-A209DAEF9AE9'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What fraction of the shape is shaded?

![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg)
 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {12} {16} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 4} {4 \times 4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times \cancel {4}} {4 \times \cancel {4}} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {4}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {3} {4}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {12} {16} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 4} {4 \times 4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times \cancel {4}} {4 \times \cancel {4}} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {4}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-55EDBB73-0F13-4A5E-B7C4-543D1C506A2B'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Work out $\dfrac{5}{8}$ of $48$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac{5}{8} \text{ of } 48 \\\\
&= \dfrac{5}{8} \times 48 \\\\
&= \dfrac{5 \times 48}{8} \\\\
&= \dfrac{5 \times 6 \times 8}{8} \\\\
&= \dfrac{5 \times 6 \times \cancel{8}}{\cancel{8}} \\\\
&= 5 \times 6  \\\\
&= 30
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$30$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac{5}{8} \text{ of } 48 \\\\
&= \dfrac{5}{8} \times 48 \\\\
&= \dfrac{5 \times 48}{8} \\\\
&= \dfrac{5 \times 6 \times 8}{8} \\\\
&= \dfrac{5 \times 6 \times \cancel{8}}{\cancel{8}} \\\\
&= 5 \times 6  \\\\
&= 30
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$30$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-45A231A8-13FE-4D37-B71A-F07B391EDE00'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Work out $\dfrac{2}{3}$  of $81$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac{2}{3} \ \text {of} \ 81 \\\\
&= \dfrac {2} {3} \times 81 \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 81} {3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 27 \times 3} {3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 27 \times \cancel {3}} {\cancel {3}} \\\\
&= 2 \times 27 \\\\
&= 54
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$54$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac{2}{3} \ \text {of} \ 81 \\\\
&= \dfrac {2} {3} \times 81 \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 81} {3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 27 \times 3} {3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 27 \times \cancel {3}} {\cancel {3}} \\\\
&= 2 \times 27 \\\\
&= 54
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$54$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

Kate asked some people to tell her their favourite character in the Wizard of Oz.

Here is a pie chart showing her results:

</div>
<div class='workings'>

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What percentage of the people she asked liked Toto best ?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{\% people that liked Toto best}       &= \dfrac {2} {8} \times 100 \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {2 \times 100} {8} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {2 \times 25 \times 4} {2 \times 4} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 25 \times \cancel {4}} {\cancel {2} \times \cancel {4}} \\\\
                                            &= 25 \%
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$25 \%$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{\% people that liked Toto best}       &= \dfrac {2} {8} \times 100 \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {2 \times 100} {8} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {2 \times 25 \times 4} {2 \times 4} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 25 \times \cancel {4}} {\cancel {2} \times \cancel {4}} \\\\
                                            &= 25 \%
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$25 \%$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

$24$ people said that they liked Dorothy best.

How many more people liked Dorothy than the Scarecrow?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the total number of people $=a$

Number of people that liked scarecrow $=\dfrac{a}{8}$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac {4} {8} \ \text {of} \ a                 &= 24 \\\\
\dfrac {4} {8} \times a                         &= 24 \\\\
a                                               &= \dfrac {24 \times 8} {4} \\\\
a                                               &= \dfrac {24 \times 4 \times 2} {4} \\\\
a                                               &= \dfrac {24 \times \cancel{4} \times 2} {\cancel{4}} \\\\
a                                               &= 24 \times  2 \\\\
a                                               &= 48
\end{aligned}
$

Total number of people $= 48$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{People that liked scarecrow}              &= \dfrac{a}{8} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac{48}{8} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {6 \times 8} {8} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {6 \times \cancel{8}} {\cancel{8}} \\\\
                                                &= 6 \\\\
\text {Difference}                              &= 24 - 6 \\\\
                                                &= 18
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$18$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let the total number of people $=a$

Number of people that liked scarecrow $=\dfrac{a}{8}$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac {4} {8} \ \text {of} \ a                 &= 24 \\\\
\dfrac {4} {8} \times a                         &= 24 \\\\
a                                               &= \dfrac {24 \times 8} {4} \\\\
a                                               &= \dfrac {24 \times 4 \times 2} {4} \\\\
a                                               &= \dfrac {24 \times \cancel{4} \times 2} {\cancel{4}} \\\\
a                                               &= 24 \times  2 \\\\
a                                               &= 48
\end{aligned}
$

Total number of people $= 48$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{People that liked scarecrow}              &= \dfrac{a}{8} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac{48}{8} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {6 \times 8} {8} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {6 \times \cancel{8}} {\cancel{8}} \\\\
                                                &= 6 \\\\
\text {Difference}                              &= 24 - 6 \\\\
                                                &= 18
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$18$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-334659B0-F886-4B5E-B1FD-FF0FA7CB2959'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Work out two thirds of $54$. 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {2} {3} \ \text{of} \ 54 \\\\
&= \dfrac {2}{3} \times 54 \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 54}{3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 18 \times 3} {3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 18 \times \cancel{3}} {\cancel{3}} \\\\
&= 2 \times 18 \\\\
&= 36
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$36$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {2} {3} \ \text{of} \ 54 \\\\
&= \dfrac {2}{3} \times 54 \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 54}{3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 18 \times 3} {3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 18 \times \cancel{3}} {\cancel{3}} \\\\
&= 2 \times 18 \\\\
&= 36
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$36$
</code></pre>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-14F26701-F24D-405B-AE41-7F50EFF71BAB'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Work out $\dfrac{3}{5}$ of $45$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&=  \dfrac {3} {5} \ \text {of} \ 45 \\\\
&=  \dfrac {3} {5} \times 45 \\\\
&=  \dfrac {3 \times 45} {5} \\\\
&=  \dfrac {3 \times 9 \times 5} {5} \\\\
&=  \dfrac {3 \times 9 \times \cancel {5}} {\cancel {5}} \\\\
&=  3 \times 9 \\\\
&=  27
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$27$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&=  \dfrac {3} {5} \ \text {of} \ 45 \\\\
&=  \dfrac {3} {5} \times 45 \\\\
&=  \dfrac {3 \times 45} {5} \\\\
&=  \dfrac {3 \times 9 \times 5} {5} \\\\
&=  \dfrac {3 \times 9 \times \cancel {5}} {\cancel {5}} \\\\
&=  3 \times 9 \\\\
&=  27
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$27$
</code></pre>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-EA1B0CA0-D56A-4468-B448-41B64C640240'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Work out $\dfrac{3}{4}$ of $72$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {3} {4} \ \text {of} \ 72 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {4} \times 72 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 72}{4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 18 \times 4} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 18 \times \cancel{4}} {\cancel{4}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 18 \\\\
&= 54
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$54$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {3} {4} \ \text {of} \ 72 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {4} \times 72 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 72}{4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 18 \times 4} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 18 \times \cancel{4}} {\cancel{4}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 18 \\\\
&= 54
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$54$
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class='question subquestion'>

Work out one fifth of $180$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&=  \dfrac {1} {5} \times 180 \\\\
&=  \dfrac {180} {5} \\\\
&=  \dfrac {36 \times 5} {5} \\\\
&=  \dfrac {36 \times \cancel {5}} {\cancel {5}} \\\\
&=  36
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$36$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&=  \dfrac {1} {5} \times 180 \\\\
&=  \dfrac {180} {5} \\\\
&=  \dfrac {36 \times 5} {5} \\\\
&=  \dfrac {36 \times \cancel {5}} {\cancel {5}} \\\\
&=  36
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class='workings'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&=  \dfrac {5} {8} \ \text {of} \ 96 \\\\
&=  \dfrac {5} {8} \times 96 \\\\
&=  \dfrac {5 \times 96} {8} \\\\
&=  \dfrac {5 \times 12 \times 8} {8} \\\\
&=  \dfrac {5 \times 12 \times \cancel {8}} {\cancel {8}} \\\\
&=  5 \times 12 \\\\
&=  60
\end{aligned}
$

&=  \dfrac {5 \times 12 \times \cancel {8}} {\cancel {8}} \\\\

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-0DA91A78-9D3B-401D-88F2-BF5050E8FEF5'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Work out $\dfrac{3}{8}$ of $72$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac{3}{8} \ \text{of}\ 72 \\\\
&= \dfrac{3}{8} \times 72 \\\\
&= \dfrac{3 \times 72}{8} \\\\
&= \dfrac{3 \times 8 \times 9} {8} \\\\
&= \dfrac{3 \times 9 \times \cancel{8}} {\cancel{8}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 9 \\\\
&= 27
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$27$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac{3}{8} \ \text{of}\ 72 \\\\
&= \dfrac{3}{8} \times 72 \\\\
&= \dfrac{3 \times 72}{8} \\\\
&= \dfrac{3 \times 8 \times 9} {8} \\\\
&= \dfrac{3 \times 9 \times \cancel{8}} {\cancel{8}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 9 \\\\
&= 27
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$27$
</code></pre>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-18DB7E66-9E11-40BF-A69B-1B5CDE9C342B'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Work out $\dfrac{3}{4}$ of $96$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {3} {4} \ \text {of} \ 96 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {4} \times 96 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 96} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 24 \times 4} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 24 \times \cancel{4}} {\cancel{4}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 24 \\\\
&= 72
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$72$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {3} {4} \ \text {of} \ 96 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {4} \times 96 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 96} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 24 \times 4} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 24 \times \cancel{4}} {\cancel{4}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 24 \\\\
&= 72
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
